- **deepline-analytics**: Use this skill when answering business analytics, RevOps, GTM metric, pipeline, revenue, funnel, customer, or warehouse questions with Deepline. Skip prospecting, enrichment, contact finding, outbound, or personalization workflows; use deepline-gtm for those.
